   a CS addi temp (first occurrence per node = direct d-form);
   cast-blocked second-pointer mediation splits one site per +1 id,
   two mediations spill. NEGATIVE on idiom 117: a volatile-cast read
   pins the LOAD, not the address web.
138. **DEPTH-0 AUTO-INLINE IS SIZE-GATED (refines 123; lbaudio
   27648)**: large loop-bearing plain globals do NOT depth-0
   auto-inline (bl emitted) — the depth-0 exception holds only for
   small callees.
139. **EXPORT-SCOPE SOURCE FIX (extends 120/133; gm_1BFA w21,
   byte-proven)**: EXPORT-MISSING row + symbols.txt scope:global ⇒
   the decomp wrongly made it `static` — drop the static; sections
   stay byte-identical (only .comment moves) and the row clears free.
   Audit ALL importer-cleared EXPORT-MISSING rows against symbols.txt
   scope.
140. ~~**ANCHOR-PAIR IS A SLOT LAW (refines 129; cs-law w21)**~~
   **DISSOLVED w23 (idioms 171/172)**: at low pressure processing is
   pure decl order under L1' — the "slot park" class cannot exist
   there, and the p09/p10 "inverter mechanism" anomaly was an
   artifact of the falsified cursor rule. Surviving content: anon
   anchors process right after the const/call-temp chain (no decl
   perm moves them below a named single); gm-class parks reduce to
   ONE anon-vs-named precedence, attackable via idiom 173.
141. **TAIL-MERGE TRAP (extends 73/83; itdraw V6 47.59)**:
   byte-identical sibling expansions from ONE static inline get
   cross-jumped (MWCC tail-merges them) — duplicated target code must
   stay duplicated in source.
142. **TU-POSITION FALSIFIED FOR REGALLOC (sharpens L5; cs-law w21)**:
   L5 sensitivity is linkage/symbol context, never function position
   or vreg counters — prepending 1-3 dummy fns (incl. a 15-CS-web
   monster) leaves coloring byte-identical; an extern-buf reshape
   moves it.
143. **SCOPE-OVERLAY FRAME LAW (extends 127d/e; logf →100, real+
   synthetic probes)**: sibling-scope locals OVERLAY one unified
   lexical top-down frame band (scalars+aggregates together — refines
   127e's separate-bands claim); a case-block local hides 12B (cast-
   init: 4B home + 8B cast staging) / 4B (copy-class init) / 0B
   (inline use) of frame depending on init class — frame diffs can
   come from a DIFFERENT scope than the visible slots. An inline
   switch-head cast read front-end-CSEs a case-local cast-init down
   to copy-class (12B→4B). Companion: address-taken u32 scalar pair
   pins int webs (127f).
144. **ALIAS-CLASS SCHEDULING EDGES (extends 127; logf probes)**:
   local ARRAY stores carry may-alias edges against global loads
   (scheduler orders them); scalar and cast-pointer-to-local stores
   do NOT — array-vs-scalar staging choice is a zero-byte scheduling
   lever.
145. ~~**FPR TEMP-POOL LAW — L1 DOES NOT TRANSFER**~~ **FALSIFIED as
   mechanism w24 (fpr-retest-w24) — REPLACED by idioms 183-185.**
   "Virgin-descending in creation order" was reverse-order processing
   on an all-interfering clique read forwards. The true rule is
   ascending first-fit WITH dirty reuse (183). SURVIVING pieces: the
   EC4C C-unreachable VERDICT (re-confirmed via 3 independent walls —
   see FPR-LAW VERDICT) and obs.2 (detached-result → highest dead
   volatile, now idiom 185). Adjacent result fadds dest=src2 coalesce
   and named-multi-def L3 promotion still hold.
146. **NAMED-DEF POOL-SLOT INTERLEAVING (extends 122/127b, closes
   118's mid-pool gap; ftPr_SpecialN + itlinkbomb, byte-proven)**:
   file-scope `const f32` defs emit into .sdata2 at their LEXICAL
   position INTERLEAVED with anonymous pool literals in creation
   order — between-fns def lands between those fns' pool windows,
   top-of-file at HEAD before all pool literals, EOF at tail.
   FLIP-grade pool-position creator: zero .text, named export
   restored free.
147. **FLOAT CONST-PROP CAST CURE (extends 127b to floats, narrows
   127c)**: a visible-init const f32 referenced by NAME folds to an
   anonymous pool DUP under melee `-str reuse` too; `*(f32*)&sym`
   re-type read defeats the fold with byte-identical lfs (volatile
   NOT required — both probed). 146+147 together retarget any
   literal's pool slot to any lexical point.
148. **DEAD-CREATOR DEATH IS UNIVERSAL PRE-POOLING (extends 118)**:
   fully-dead locals AND param-gated inline branches folded
   post-substitution both die PRE-pooling — no anonymous dead creator
   exists from C; idiom 146/147 is the only flip-grade pool-position
   route (mechanism behind lb_00CE's C-unreachable verdict).
149. **SR-ROVER RECON TELL (grgreatbay 63F4 →100)**: decomp loops of
   the form `arr[off/K]` + `off += K` are decompiler-INVENTED —
   direct `arr[i]` lets MWCC create the target's strength-reduction
   rovers, incl. the `addi rR,rI,0` rover-init of a known-zero i
   (not a user copy) and hit-branch `slwi` recomputes.
150. **HAND-SIM PREDICTIVE AT ≤7 WEBS (grgreatbay 63F4; w23-updated)**:
   hand-simulating the choice rule over the decl order predicted a
   full 4-CS-reg map BEFORE compiling (first fully-predicted S1 win)
   — simulate before probing. ⚠ w23: simulate **L1' (idiom 171) +
   pure decl order (172)**, NOT the falsified cursor model (the 63F4
   prediction survived because the chain was short).
151. **MUTATION-DEMOTION IS NON-COMPOSITIONAL (refines 34; grgreatbay
   5460)**: idiom-34 self-read demotions do NOT compose across
   subsets — a `+0` form dissolves where the cast form acts; probe
   the exact subset, never extrapolate from singles.
152. **PER-SITE INLINE-AGGREGATE STREAM (extends 31/41/49; sobjlib
   mechanism)**: expanded-inline aggregates + arg copies form
   bottom-band per-site groups — one descending stream of [arg-copy
   above staging] per call site in instantiation order; reproducible
   with local static-inline wrappers, but the stream bottom-pins
   (dead-bottom fill below it = the cobj/lbColl parked family).
153. **CALL-BEARING INLINE 8B CHARGE — FIELD-CONFIRMED (extends 49;
   grgreatbay 499C)**: a call-bearing helper reproduced the target's
   two Vec3 slots exactly but paid +0x10 frame; depth-2 re-routing
   does NOT drop the 8B charge (the depth-2 hole-kill of idiom 49 is
   sqrtf-hole-specific).
154. **COPY-INIT BLOCK LOCAL FORWARDS INTO THE CALL SLOT (extends
   143/152; sobjlib 803A4A68 37→25 rows, w22)**: for memory-sourced
   by-value aggregate args, `{ T c = mem; call(c); }` emits ONE store,
   forwards the local into the call-arg slot at the EXACT target
   offset, and pools dead home space (7 GXSetTevColor sites relocated
   to 0x34..0x4C in one shot); direct calls / param-form wrappers
   bottom-pin in the zone instead. First C-reachable lever that moves
   call-temp bands. TevColorS10/KColor variant: `static inline`
   wrappers with cast-init internal locals (152-class groups).
155. **CASE-BLOCK LOCALS STACK + CAST-CLASS→INLINE-USE CURE (field-
   confirms 143's ladder; grgreatbay 499C →99.99, w22)**: sibling
   case-block scopes do NOT overlay — they stack lexically top-down;
   a case-block cast-class local hides 12B of frame, folding it into
   direct call arguments (inline-use class) hides 0B — releasing
   exactly the bytes the per-case Vec3 slots needed. Fn-scope local →
   per-case block locals relocates the slots.
156. **FRAME BAND-MIRROR PARK (sharpens the cobj/lbColl family;
   sobjlib w22)**: ours bottom-up [zone groups][forwarded slots][dead
   pool] vs target [dead pool][forwarded slots][groups]; zone
   reservation == dead pool byte-for-byte (0x28). Falsified spellings
   (do NOT re-probe): cast-init blocks (non-compositional, 151),
   pointer/param/register/u8-array wrapper params, cast-deref args,
   double-local, trailing/sized PAD blocks, helper def order/position/
   plain-static, forward defs. Synthetic harnesses reproduce group
   structure but NOT real-fn band order (L5 extends to frame bands).
157. **BACKEND CONVERSION MAGICS POOL AFTER FRONT-END LITERALS
   (extends 146; itpikachuthunder .sdata2 →byte-exact, w22)**: the
   s32-cast f64 0x4330 magic pools at BACKEND lowering after ALL
   front-end f32 literals and is un-name-claimable — cure by REMOVING
   the colliding f32 from the anonymous pool via a named def (146) +
   `*(f32*)&` read (147); the magic then lands at its natural aligned
   slot.
158. **INT-LAUNDERED SELF-ASSIGN IS THE ONLY SINGLE-WEB FOLD BLOCKER
   (extends 34/136; lbMemory_80015320 96.1→98.6, w22)**:
   `p = (T*)((u32)p + K);` keeps the target's self-addi + d-form
   +0x28.. addressing; plain/&-form/u8*+=/cast-only/+0/volatile forms
   all fold; a separately-laundered variable splits the web.
159. **INT-LAUNDER ONE OCCURRENCE TO SPLIT A CROSS-CALL ADDRESS NODE
   (corollary of 137; lbmemory w22)**: storing via
   `*(u32*)((u32)p + 0x30)` keeps load/store as TWO address nodes,
   killing the extra CS addi temp the repeat law would otherwise pin.
160. **KNOWN-CONST STORE REMAT IS PROP-INVINCIBLE (itpikachuthunder
   DF8 17B park, w22; ~25 probes)**: a target li that feeds a store
   AND zero-shares with a live web is C-unreachable when the value is
   a known constant — chain/cast/+0/register/volatile/width forms all
   canonicalize; undef-read binds the web but adds clrlwi/extsh.
   Surfaces only with ≥2 const-store uses sharing one li
   (grBigBlueRoute precedent); whole-game scan: pattern UNIQUE to DF8.
161. **CHAINED-ASSIGNMENT OPAQUE ZERO (ftmetal w22; donor
   ftAnim_8006F994 matched)**: `a = b = sp = 0;` — outer chain vars
   become opaque copies (SR-rover/shift inits do NOT fold: `slwi
   rD,rS,4` survives from known-zero rS); the innermost stays literal
   (folds to li 0); the stack store CSEs onto the innermost local's
   reg. Chain direction selects fold/CSE per variable — cures the
   `li r0,0 / li rN,0 / slwi` prologue family (ftKb-style fns).
162. **NESTED-RATE DOUBLE ROVER (ftmetal ft_800C85B8 →100, w22)**:
   `base[ctr]` in an inner loop with ctr surviving the outer loop
   creates an outer slwi-init rover + per-inner `mr` working copy;
   both anonymous → processed BEFORE named locals (explains
   byte_off-above-fp maps). Hand-named offset pairs rank wrong.
163. **VOID-INLINE PROMOTION (extends 37; ifprize w22)**: moving a web
   cluster into a `static inline void` helper promotes its named webs
   ABOVE anonymous temp chains at ZERO frame cost; a value-returning
   helper pays a +8B result-slot band (not removable by direct-return
   when a fall-off path exists; `register`/scope-overlay inert);
   an identity helper promotes nothing. **w29 REFINEMENT (upstream
   itdraw)**: the void-inline also works as a DEDUP of duplicated
   control flow that DELETES the residual web's defining statement
   rather than recoloring it — see 257. ⚠ the "`register` inert"
   parenthetical is style-divergent vs upstream (their matched logf
   carries `register` on both float temps); cheap 1-compile check
   queued.
164. **SDATA2 SPLIT-BOUNDARY LAW (ifprize w22; splits.txt 5-way
   re-split, DOL byte-proven)**: importer-clustered `un_*` pool runs
   inside a lumped .sdata2 split = ORIGINAL per-TU pool boundaries —
   re-split splits.txt instead of extern-read contortions (manual
   0x4330 conversion CANNOT bind an extern from C: fsub+frsp never
   contracts to fsubs). 804DDBA8–DCC8 → ifprize/ifcoget/soundtest/
   textdraw/textlib; killed 12 reloc rows with PLAIN literals and
   fixed if_2FC93's SIZE:.sdata2 row for free.
165. **NAMED-CONST POOL-RECON DEFS MUST BE NON-STATIC (pool-order
   sweep w22, fox)**: idiom-146-class defs only claim pool slots as
   non-static globals; int literals sitting in f32 ARG positions count
   as pool entries; INT-param literals must STAY literal; f64 M_PI is
   definable this way.
166. **HEADGAP LAW — head-side ALIGNFIX dual (fox gate, w22; ⭐
   link/DOL-PROVEN w23 by flip #13)**: dtk absorbs a predecessor's
   align pad into the NEXT unit's section head; the pad restores at
   link via align-8 (fox: byte-exact at the DOL). Gate such units with
   `pool-order-w22/gate_headgap.py` + the predecessor-end condition
   (fox: ftKb_SpecialNPk dtk object ends 804D944C) + link-order
   adjacency, not raw whole-object equality; final proof = DOL sha.
167. **LITERAL-COMPARE OPERAND CANON (extends 50; pool-order sweep
   w22)**: target compares emit const-FIRST (`k == x3`) — literal
   operand order is codegen-visible; use const-first when the target
   shows it.
168. **BSS/SBSS COMMONS EMISSION LAW (ftdevice 36-object .bss recon,
   w22; sharpens the wave-3 rule, cross-confirmed by ifprize .sbss)**:
   statics emit at decl order first, then UNREFERENCED commons flush
   in REVERSE decl order; `={0}` moves the object to .data. THE lever
   for whole-section SIZE rows (0x30→0xc58 in one recon).
169. **CROSS-UNIT JTBL FLIP-BLOCKER (ftKb_SpecialNZd class, w22)**: a
   jumptable owned by another unit's section makes the residual
   C-unreachable from this TU — park, don't fight.
170. **FUZZY-INVISIBLE CALL-TARGET BUGS (itwstar w22; extends 133 and
   the idiom-88 hard rule)**: REL24 callee identity is invisible to
   fuzzy-100 scoring — itwstar's Logic29 called it_80294364 where the
   target calls it_80294624. Audit REL24s on every fuzzy-100 unit
   before flip; linkcheck.py catches it, fuzzy does not.
171. **⭐⭐ L1' — CS CHOICE RULE IS LOWEST-DIRTY-FIRST (slotlaw-inverter
   w23; REPLACES/FALSIFIES 128)**: scan DIRTY callee-saved regs in
   ASCENDING numeric order (r25 before r26 … r31), take the first
   whose holders don't interfere; else the HIGHEST VIRGIN reg. NO
   cursor, NO descending dirty scan. Validated: 22/22 synthetic
   byte-fits (incl. every cursor-era "anomaly") + 44/44 w20 replica
   colorings + all 3 gm_801BFCFC maps (l1prime.py/mine44.py/solve3.py
   in slotlaw-inverter-w23/). All cursor-artifact reasoning and the
   w20/w21 order corpora are VOID.
172. **⭐ LOW-PRESSURE PROCESSING = PURE DECL ORDER (corollary of 171;
   sharpens 1/131, DISSOLVES 140)**: with no anonymous webs, decl
   perms reach EVERY L1'-admissible coloring — a complete lever; the
   "slot park" class cannot exist below the promotion regime.
   Re-triage all parked transposition pairs against L1' predictions
   first. High-pressure promotion is decl-NON-monotonic (slot-0
   demotes, decl-late tail/count promote, slot-2/3 neighbors flip
   slot-1, call-temps decl-inert) — quantitative law OPEN, 44 labeled
   points ready.
173. **ROVER ANONYMIZATION MOVES THE ANCHOR PAIR (extends 149/162; gm
   real-fn byte-proven, w23)**: replacing decomp-invented walker
   pointers with direct `base[idx]` indexing converts the walker web
   to an SR rover, REORDERING the (anchor, walker) allocation pair —
   the first C-reachable lever that moved gm_801BFCFC's BASE to its
   target register (98.25). Cost: the rover processes phase-0. ⚠ w24:
   all intra-file demotion forms falsified — phase-0 promotion is a
   MODE (197) and the gm demote lever is header-owned (198).
174. **CONST-STORE ZERO-WEB THRESHOLD (proves/extends 160; g01/g02/g05
   synthetic, w23)**: a shared zero becomes a register web only at
   ≥2 STORE uses (call-crossing pair → CS reg; adjacent no-call pair
   → r0); a live variable's own `=0` init NEVER joins the store web
   from plain C. The itpikachuthunder DF8 prologue (single sth
   sharing the live web's li) is thereby proven a park.
175. **SJISWRAP NON-ASCII TRAP TAXONOMY (hygiene w23, bytegate-proven
   ×7)**: src is UTF-8 and sjiswrap converts UTF-8→SJIS at compile;
   non-ASCII inside string literals is INTENTIONAL Japanese — never
   touch; the trap class is typography in COMMENTS (U+2014 em-dash,
   U+00D7, U+00B0, stray kana) — replace with ASCII; comment-only
   fixes are byte-neutral (cmp-identical .o). Scanners must NOT do
   SJIS lead-byte skipping (UTF-8 trail bytes are never ASCII;
   lead-skip misparses and false-flags strings).
176. **OURS-EXTRA UNREFERENCED SECTION IS DEADSTRIPPED (sharpens
   116/134; ftmetal flip #10 DOL byte-proof, w23)**: an ours-only
   section with no relocs into it, no exported syms, no force_active,
   inside a KEPT object is mwld-deadstripped at link — benign for
   flips. TOOLING GAP: stock linkcheck.py iterates TARGET sections
   only and never reports OURS-EXTRA — a REFERENCED ours-extra
   section would silently break the DOL; rank.py should add the row.
177. **ENTRY-ANCHOR PREPEND LAW (texp p01-p10, w23; sharpens 15/56 —
   the mr-vs-addi "compiler-internal" singleton class is an
   IR-POSITION tell)**: GCSE-merged pooled-constant anchors land at
   the END of the entry BB; only LICM from a loop whose preheader IS
   the entry BB PREPENDS the lis above the param homes — and the
   early IR slot flips copy mnemonics mr→addi-0. while+break triggers
   all three phenomena but pays jump-to-test rotation;
   do-while(0)/for(;;)break/trip-1 loops dissolve BEFORE LICM (no
   trigger). TExpAssignReg parked on this (98.30, 6 rows; do-not-
   re-probe list in s1-texp-ifcoget-w23/REPORT.md).
178. **VOID-INLINE CS RE-RANK (extends 37/57/163; won fn_802FF218
   r30↔r31, w23)**: idiom 57's expansion re-rank applies to
   CALLEE-SAVED webs of a void static-inline expanded in caller
   position — first-decl-highest INSIDE the expansion; cracks
   fn-scope slot-inert transpositions at zero frame cost (void = 0B;
   value-returning variant charges +8B even with a single direct
   return — measured both ways).
179. **NAMED-ZERO GAP CLAIM (extends 146/165; ifcoget .sdata2
   byte-exact, w23)**: a dtk `gap_NN` 4-byte zero slot between pool
   entries is claimable by a positioned dead NON-static
   `const f32 name = 0.0f;` — it does NOT dedup against the anonymous
   0.0f pool entry (complement of 105's unreachable stream heads —
   this one IS reachable).
180. **VARARG INT-ARG addi NEEDS EXPANSION (extends 56; fn_802FF218,
   w23)**: `mr rArg,rCS` becomes `addi rArg,rCS,0` iff the value
   travels as an inline-expansion parameter; the node stays at the
   expansion's IR slot and CANNOT cross into the condition BB from an
   arm-resident call. All disp-copy spellings (plain/+0/int-laundered/
   ptr-laundered/self-read/assignment-in-condition) VN-canonicalize.
181. **BRANCH-ARM F2I STAGING-ZONE RESERVATION (ftCo AC5A0 law
   candidate, 14 probes, w23; frame analog of 118/148, park family
   156)**: branch-arm float→int conversions reserve a 3-slot frame
   staging zone whose FIRST-created slot dies — the phantom 8B is NOT
   sqrtf-owned (survives sqrtf removal and every local/scope/
   init-class/register/type/cast/inline-depth spelling); it dies ONLY
   when the f2i conversions become straight-line single-BB. Do not
   re-probe the listed spellings.
182. **GRAV-POINTER BLOCKED-DEREF RECIPE (extends 42/46; won ftCo
   A9904 +1.10 / A9CB4 +0.63, w23)**: target tell = `addi rP` then
   `lfs lit@sda21` then `lfs 0x16c(rBase)` order with a later
   cross-BB `lfs 0x0(r3)` ⇒ build a blocked pointer
   (`temp = &agg.field;` + cast-only self-read) and route BOTH the
   same-BB read (folds to d-form but schedules AFTER the addi) and
   the else-arm divisor through `*temp` (one shared deref node); repay
   the @-ledger exactly (PAD_STACK→UNUSED-pad −4, `if(0){}` +2,
   `goto L; L:` +1).
183. **⭐⭐ FPR CHOICE RULE = ASCENDING FIRST-FIT + DIRTY REUSE
   (fpr-retest-w24; REWRITES 145)**: the L1' shape TRANSFERS to FPR
   volatiles — scan dirty volatile FPRs ascending, take first
   non-interfering (dirty reuse proven: q02 disjoint statements chain
   onto f1/f0; q04 reuses dirty f1 after f0 blocked); CS overflow =
   highest virgin f31 (the GPR L1' else-branch). EC4C/sobjlib ours
   AND target maps all re-derive exactly.
184. **FPR PROCESSING = REVERSE-CREATION, STATEMENT-BOUND, DYING-SRC
   TRANSPARENT (fpr-retest-w24)**: FPR temps process in REVERSE
   creation order within a statement; stores are statement-bound
   (never cross statements); a dying source is transparent to its
   consumer; f64 never shares; named multi-use webs phase AFTER anon
   singles (the "f7+ band" = 7 temps below, not a fixed band).
   **STATEMENT order, not decl order, is the FPR lever** — the FPR
   analog of idiom 172.
185. **DETACHED-RESULT WEBS TAKE HIGHEST DEAD VOLATILE (fpr-retest-
   w24; the surviving special case of old 145 obs.2)**: a result web
   detached from its expression (multi-def staging) takes the highest
   DEAD volatile; detachment requires multi-def staging, which wrecks
   the band — this is EC4C wall (3).
186. **MULTI-USE CONST-LOAD WEB PHASE IS CONTEXT-GLOBAL (fpr-retest-
   w24; sobjlib trio)**: a 2+-use float const web's processing slot
   is set by WHOLE-FN context (early in leaf fns, last in branchy
   fns), not by local spelling — 11 levers inert. Fingerprint:
   literal/visible-init spellings flip fmadds operand order
   (original sobjlib HAD named cw/ch + named-extern read).
187. **PARAM-FED vs LOCAL-DEF ORDERING FLIP (l1prime-parks-w24
   T4/T6, byte-proven; sharpens 125)**: HOW a web is fed (call
   arg/param vs internal local def) flips its processing order vs
   its sibling decl-init NULL web; byte-shape invariant, ORDER not.
   This IS itdraw V7's transposition mechanism; first-web demotion
   vanishes under call-arg coalescing.
188. **DEPTH-SHIELD IS LOAD-BEARING (proves 123 by destruction;
   itdraw T2 0.00 / T3 28.70)**: hand-expanding wrapper inlines makes
   same-TU loop callees depth-0 → `-inline auto` inlines them
   loops-and-all (+~0xA0 stack). NEVER flatten decomp-invented
   inlines; never share one def across branches.
189. **SLOT-0 DEMOTION / DECL-LAST PROMOTION ON REAL FNS, WITH
   IMMUNITIES (extends mine44; lbmemory L1/F1)**: plain decl-0
   mid-fn-def webs DEMOTE out of the long class; decl-LAST promotes
   to class front. IMMUNE: first-statement webs (alloc), loop-carried
   webs, homed params. Dead-source param copies are web-identity
   no-ops. Also: N/Z-def statements reading a param web interfere
   with it (path-sensitive, statement-granular liveness).
190. **NAMED MULTI-DEF SELF-ASSIGN IS THE ONLY ONE-REGISTER
   DESTRUCTION (sharpens 158; lbmemory L2)**: anon-base + fresh
   pointer spellings emit addi-temp + copy (extra instr). Plain anon
   @ha/@l anchors at HIGH pressure process AFTER the named-long class
   (f01's anchor-first is low-pressure-only).
191. **IDIOM-172 COMPLETENESS BOUNDARY (bounds 172; lbmemory F1+L1)**:
   above the promotion threshold, decl perms only permute named
   non-immune locals WITHIN their class band — params, anchors,
   first-statement webs, call-temps are PINNED. Before promising a
   decl solve, check the park's forced diff is confined to permutable
   webs.
192. **⭐ CHAIN-PHASE LAW (sharpens 171/172; ifprize win, w24)**:
   anchor-derived address-chain webs allocate as ONE LEADING PHASE —
   after expansion-named webs, before all caller-named webs — in def
   order, named OR anon; decl moves, naming, multi-def merging, and
   idiom-34 launders are ALL inert on chains. The C lever is
   idiom-163 promotion of the contended SCALAR into a void
   static-inline expansion (it becomes expansion-named and allocates
   before the chains). Won un_802FE918 →99.94.
193. **VALUE-INLINE WEB-SPLIT (extends 163/178; ifprize probes)**: a
   value-returning inline's result web coalesces with internal
   mr-arms, but scratch-var reuse across a dead range is web-SPLIT;
   the +8B frame charge applies even at one call site.
194. **PARAM-LEAD TOGGLE (ifprize probes, w24)**: the param web leads
   CS processing order UNLESS decl-1's initializer contains an inline
   call — then the param goes LAST.
195. **GP-MUTATION POISON / DEMOTE-TO-LAST (sharpens 151/34;
   grgreatbay 5460, 17 probes)**: a single idiom-34-mutated web
   demotes to LAST (cast == +0, placement-inert); but mutating a web
   whose init is an inline call yields a FIXED scrambled coloring AND
   suppresses co-mutations — mutation demotion is not just
   non-compositional (151), it can be provably unable to reach a
   target trail-pair.
196. **GET_JOBJ-ON-CS CHARGE (extends 163/153; kongo-shape transfer
   test)**: a value-returning accessor on a CS-live jobj charges +8B
   frame (kongo's pads = the absorbed band); frame-exact targets
   without the band EXCLUDE that spelling.
197. **ANON PHASE-0 PROMOTION IS A MODE, NOT A CLASS (rover-demote-
   w24)**: WHICH anon web promotes to phase-0 flips on byte-neutral
   deltas — subword param type at an extsh'd call site, v26-vs-
   {i3,w2} decl order, walker-web deletion (moves promotion onto the
   anchor). Do not reason about "the rover always promotes".
198. **⭐ SUBWORD-PARAM DEMOTE LEVER (rover-demote-w24, qr9
   byte-proof)**: a callee PROTOTYPE's first param being subword
   (`short`) vs enum/bool DEMOTES the caller's SR rover out of
   phase-0 AND produces gm_801BFCFC's entire target map — byte-
   neutral modulo the CS permutation. Header-owned: the lever lives
   in toy.h, not the caller's TU; call-site cast spellings
   ((s32)(s16)/(s16)/s16-temp/bool) are ALL inert. toy.c's own
   `(s16) trophyId` casts suggest the original header WAS subword.
199. **PX-REPLICA PROTOTYPE TRAP (rover-demote-w24; extends L5/142)**:
   standalone replicas must replicate callee PROTOTYPES exactly —
   a wrong param type silently flips the phase-0 promotion mode
   (197/198) and the replica "solves" a different function. The w23
   rover-demotion queue came from exactly this trap.
200. **⭐⭐ PROMOTION-BAR LAW (lawmine-w25; quantifies 171-promotion/
   197; cracks the high-pressure order law for the tail web)**: web X
   promotes iff **eff(X) ≥ K_X − W**, where eff(X) = # real named
   webs declared before X that don't themselves promote (call-temps
   excluded) and W = # short→int widening temp vregs at call sites.
   K_tail=5 proven at W=0/1/2 both-side boundaries (t6/t7, o1/o2,
   u5/u6 causal probes); bars shift 1:1 per widening site,
   position-INDEPENDENT. Promoted webs process in decl order above
   the temp chain. Replica i3 contributes +1 extra (weight-form
   proven over OR-form; the call-arg-0 mechanism falsified — open).
201. **WIDENING ASYMMETRY (lawmine-w25; sharpens 198/200)**: only
   short→int WIDENING param sites count toward W; int→short
   narrowing re-types with int args shift nothing. W is only
   lowerable via subword params on already-subword-valued args —
   header-owned in every real case so far.
202. **ROVER TWO-STATE (lawmine-w25, real-body probes)**:
   gm_801BFCFC's rover promotes iff W≥2; the W=0 and W=1 maps are
   BYTE-IDENTICAL = the full target map — removing ONE widening
   suffices (toy.h-class s16 overshoots safely; both gm_1BFA sites
   are Trophy calls, so the prototype is still the unique lever).
203. **COUNT/V26 NON-BOOLEAN BOUND (lawmine-w25)**: count/v26
   promotion is NOT any 1-2-term boolean rule over 325 order/
   position/count atoms across 63 labeled rows (machine-exhausted,
   rulemine2/4). B(i3) uniquely fit all 44 corpus rows yet was
   falsified CAUSALLY (m02) — corpus perms are CORRELATED;
   fresh-context probes are MANDATORY before declaring single-atom
   laws. Promotion membership is perm-dependent and richer than
   {tail,count,v26}.
204. **⭐ SUBWORD LEVER IS TU-LOCAL-SCOPEABLE (toyh-retype-w25;
   sharpens 198; landed gm_801BFCFC →100.0000)**: idiom-198's lever
   does NOT need header ownership — if the include is direct and the
   TU uses no other symbol from that header, dropping the include +
   a TU-local subword prototype reproduces the qr9 map (same REL24).
   The w24 "conflicting local redecl" blocker only applies while the
   header stays included.
205. **GLOBAL SUBWORD RE-TYPE REGRESSES INT-CONSUMING CALLERS
   (toyh-retype-w25; falsifies gm_1BFA Path A by 7-TU bytegate)**:
   mwcc shorts are LAZILY extended — extsh-def'd short webs are
   reused; copy-def'd short webs copy-prop away and remat extsh per
   int-use (toy.c 100→98.97, tyfigupon −0.69 + frame −0x50→−0x58,
   gmMainLib −0.43). Prototype re-type verdicts are PER-CALL-SITE;
   divergent per-TU extern decls (gm_1BFA subword vs toy/tyfigupon/
   gmmain_lib int-class) are plausibly AUTHENTIC to the original
   tree. toy.h keeps (enum_t, bool) — never re-probe.
206. **INTRA-ANCHOR CHAIN LIFO (s1-w25, gmopening; sharpens 192)**:
   SAME-anchor derived temps process REVERSE-def-order in ours
   (target = def order); ifprize's def-order datum was CROSS-anchor.
   Forward/LIFO is a 197-class MODE, C-unreachable so far (~50 forms
   falsified; forced diff confined to non-permutable anon webs per
   191 → not decl-solvable).
207. **PARAM-SPLIT VN-FOLD (s1-w25, ifcoget; extends 180 + the w23
   walls)**: duplicate-value params VN-canonicalize and fold; a
   MUTATED param fed by a call result still coalesces (dying-src
   transparency on GPR expansion params) — param-splitting is CLOSED
   as a slot lever; the arm-resident-call wall stands.
208. **163-vs-160 CLOSURE (s1b-w25, itpika DF8)**: idiom-163
   void-inline promotion CANNOT defeat idiom-160 known-const store
   remat — MWCC const-prop runs POST-expansion; expansion naming
   re-ranks webs but never de-consts them, and pointer laundering
   through an expanded inline is SSA-folded. DF8 is C-unreachable
   pending a non-const-prop mechanism — removed from the L1'
   re-solve queue (BLOCKED-class).
209. **GOTO-ABORT RANGE-SCAN (s1b-w25; plbonus byte-proof →100)**:
   when every fail-branch of an unrolled range-check loop targets
   ONE label PAST the post-loop handling, the original is `goto
   skip` out of the enclosing block — NOT nested/sequential
   continue-ifs (second in-file instance: skip_combo_count). Also a
   real decomp logic-bug class: sequential-continue vs
   whole-section-abort semantics.
210. **STATIC→STATIC-INLINE IS NOT CODEGEN-NEUTRAL (s1b-w25,
   plbonus)**: adding `inline` to an already-auto-inlined plain
   `static` fn CHANGES codegen (99.84, 121B .text + 10B .data) —
   never use the inline keyword to kill a dead out-of-line copy;
   dead-copy questions belong to the deadstrip/flip gate (176).
211. **PGREP SELF-MATCH (exec-w25; sharpens the BUILD-RACE guard)**:
   plain `pgrep -f 'ninja|mwcceppc'` inside a COMPOUND zsh command
   self-matches the resident shell (single-statement invocations
   exec and don't) — a plain-pattern exit-0 in a compound command is
   NOT proof of a sibling build. Guard pattern must be
   `pgrep -f '[n]inja|[m]wcceppc'`.
212. **LOAD-FED SUBWORD ARG IS W-INERT (waudit-w26 + park-dissolve
   byte-proof; bounds 200/201)**: a subword arg consumed directly
   from a memory load (lhz/lha AT the call) contributes ZERO to W
   under both u16→u32 and u16→s32 callee re-types — 16 sites
   injected on it_8026EECC, caller coloring byte-stable both times;
   the re-typed prototype is even byte-AMBIGUOUS at the callee
   (`u16 p` == `int p` + decl-0 clamp). Only REGISTER-RESIDENT
   subword/cast values (the gm/qr9 `(s16)`-cast class) create
   bar-shifting widening vregs — consistent with idiom-205 lazy
   extension. W-audits may SKIP load-fed and literal sites; live
   idiom-180 trap: such re-types carry zero byte signal.
213. **CALLEE-BODY PARAM PIN — free instrument (waudit-w26)**: a
   100-matched callee byte-PINS its param classes (entry
   clrlwi/extsh homing + cmplw/cmpw class); re-typing wrecks the
   callee (100→90.71/89.14) before it could ever move the caller.
   Same-TU callees can NEVER take the idiom-204 TU-local-proto
   lever (the definition IS the prototype). Pin check = read the
   callee entry asm — ZERO compiles.
214. **NON-VOID RETURN TYPE PINS r3 FN-WIDE (s1-w26;
   itmewtwoshadowball byte-proof →100.0000)**: a non-void return
   type reserves r3 for the whole body even with NO return
   statement; every volatile temp chain in every inter-call region
   shifts +1. Tell: uniform one-register volatile shift across ALL
   regions of an all-regalloc fn whose param dies early. Header
   re-type is byte-neutral for non-calling includers.
215. **⭐ ROVER ANONYMIZATION REACHES PHASE PARKS (park-dissolve-w26;
   itdraw 37B→3B; extends 173/190)**: an invented NULL-init
   conditional local (`T* x = NULL; if (c) x = v;` feeding a call)
   creates a NAMED early-band web where the original passed the
   anonymous ternary `c ? v : NULL` — anonymizing it moves the web
   to the anon-late band and re-chains every downstream web. AUDIT
   ALL park residuals for invented NULL-init conditional locals
   before any order-law work.
216. **CHAINED AGGREGATE ASSIGN = PARALLEL BURST (s1-w26,
   itarwinglaser 72E0 +1.22pp; extends 161 to structs)**:
   `a = b = src;` struct chains keep member loads live across the
   chain → 2-3-reg parallel load/store burst + wider scheduling
   window; sequential copy statements serialize with dirty reuse
   (1-reg rotation). Tell: target burst uses one more simultaneous
   volatile than ours in a stack-to-stack Vec3 copy.
217. **CONSTANT-FED PARAM == CONST-INIT LOCAL (park-dissolve-w26;
   bounds 187)**: a param fed only by literal constants colors like
   a const-initialized local — param-feed is not a lever there.
218. **IDENTITY SELF-ASSIGN IS DEAD & VN-UNSPELLABLE (s1-w26 +
   park-dissolve-w26; bounds 190)**: `x = x;` / `*(u32*)&s.f =
   *(u32*)&s.f` VN-folds to the equivalent older slot — plain,
   volatile-through-cast (does NOT block VN), volatile-both
   (materializes reg addressing, +addi) and pointer-local (+8B
   frame) spellings ALL falsified; no C form emits a non-folded
   self-slot lwz/stw pair. A u8-offset LAUNDER kills a false
   cross-slot read (fixes neighbor rows) but the pair itself stays
   elided.
219. **DEPTH-2 INLINE NESTING TRAP (park-dissolve-w26, P7 88.99)**:
   wrapping an already-inlined call one level deeper wrecks the
   coloring — never add nesting depth to move a slot (companion to
   188's never-flatten).
220. **SOURCE CASE ORDER IS LAYOUT-BOUND (park-dissolve-w26, e15
   87.69)**: reordering switch cases in source moves code layout
   wholesale — case order is NOT an order-law lever.
221. **HEADER-INCLUDE @-COST (datarecon-w26, probe-isolated)**:
   `#include` of a struct-bearing header (wobj.h) alone ticks +6
   @-pool ids even with zero new code; cure = TU-local struct shape
   (204-class lever applied to types).
222. **`char x[] = "..."` DEFS DO NOT TICK @ (datarecon-w26; refines
   90/98)**: named char-array string DEFINITIONS are @-free — the
   +1 @-id cost is ANONYMOUS pooled literals only.
223. **DTK-MERGED SDATA RUNS (datarecon-w26)**: any >8B target
   .sdata "array" is really merged ≤8B scalars + pads; braced-zero
   `x[1]={0}` supplies 4B .sdata pads (restored un_804D5908 and
   returned 3 neighbors 99.8→100.0000 in soundtest).
224. **DEFINITION-VISIBILITY .TEXT COUPLING (datarecon-w26,
   probe)**: defining a previously-extern data/fn address shifts
   CONSUMER codegen; static-vs-global is indifferent — gate data
   recon with whole-unit + consumer checks, not data sections alone.
225. **ALL-ZERO >8B INITIALIZED STRUCTS DO REACH .data
   (datarecon-w26; refines the w16 ≤8B law)**: the ≤8B C-unreachable
   rule does not extend upward — explicit all-zero init of a >8B
   struct lands in .data (BF70 {0}→full HSD_CameraDescFrustum).
226. **⭐⭐ ABSOLUTE BEFORE-FORM PROMOTION BARS (lawmodel-w27;
   supersedes 200's S-relative eff-form as the CAUSAL law; closes
   the w25 W-bar hypothesis)**: a tail/count-class web promotes into
   the winner band iff its ABSOLUTE weighted decl-prefix mass (real
   named webs declared before it; replica i3 weight 2 re-confirmed
   by fitcheck2; call-temps excluded) + W clears a fixed bar —
   **K_tail=5, K_count=2**. W stays additive 1:1 (the archived
   W=1/W=2 variant rows u2-u5/o1 fit ONLY with the +W term) — the
   W-hypothesis is CLOSED: REAL as a bar term, DEAD as a park lever
   (every park audits W=0 per 212). Causally pinned on a FRESH
   5-loop/12-web body (probegen2.py; probes B0/A1-A5/B1/C1/C2/D1,
   probelog.json) — not a corpus fit. Below-bar webs can still
   promote via rescue seeding (228). ⚠ w28 bounds: K_count is
   BODY-LOCAL (242 — count promotes unconditionally on the fresh
   body); the bars govern band MEMBERSHIP only, never L1' choice
   within the band (itdraw PARK-PROVEN closure, 240).
227. **INTERFERER-PREFIX V26 GATING (lawmodel-w27)**: the v26 class
   (long-range web spanning multiple loops) is not bar-governed —
   it promotes iff its decl prefix contains webs that INTERFERE with
   its live range; non-interferers contribute nothing. Causal pair
   at the SAME decl position 1: A4 prefix {i2} (interferer) → IN vs
   A5 prefix {i1} (non-interferer) → OUT; empty-prefix B0/B1/C1/C2
   OUT; A1/A2/D1 IN. The archived "atom triggers" ({w2,i2,i4} /
   {w2,i3,w3} before v26) were extensional shadows of this rule.
   ⚠ w28 bounds: the relaxation is WHOLE-BODY-CONDITIONED —
   transport ZERO (241); always-winner webs do not satisfy the gate
   (243); empty-prefix gate is UNFIREABLE on decl-0 webs (itdraw E,
   240).
228. **WINNER-BAND RESCUE SEEDING IS UNIVERSAL (lawmodel-w27)**: a
   web below its 226-bar is RESCUED into the winner band when a web
   it interferes with is already in the band — count promotes in
   ALL 10 fresh probes including decl-0/prefix-0 (D1), seeded by the
   always-promoting tail. Constructive lever for parks: seed the
   band with an interfering winner instead of (or in addition to)
   raising the target web's prefix mass. ⚠ w28 bounds: FALSIFIED as
   count's mechanism (E1 count@0 wins with the band DEAD — count is
   unconditionally promoted, 242); on real fns the seeding DIRECTION
   is def-class-split (preamble-def promotes / mid-fn-def demotes,
   237) and the trigger intension is UNPINNED (rotation-queue item
   1); rescue is INERT on an empty band (itdraw, 240).
229. **⭐⭐ CORPUS-FIT INTENSION TRAP (lawmodel-w27; hardens 203 into
   LAW)**: a model can fit EVERY archived row and still be
   intension-wrong — the S-relative joint model (tail effB≥5 / count
   lossAfter−W≤7 / v26 atom-triggers) fit 75/75 archived rows (63
   labeled + 12 held-out W-variants) with UNIQUE fixpoints, then
   falsified on the first fresh-shape body. Archived perms are
   correlated; second offense after w25's B(i3). LAW: no
   corpus-mined rule enters the playbook without fresh-body causal
   probes on a NEW interference graph (203 discipline is MANDATORY,
   not hygiene).
230. **TWO-NAMED-WALKER PREHEADER ORDER (s1-w27; won gm_801A4014
   97.86→99.38, committed aa5934853)**: to reproduce a target
   preheader init order [li i / lbz curr / addi cur2 / addi cur]
   inside an expanded scan inline: (a) hoist a named `u8 curr` decl
   FIRST (kills the LICM-temp rotation); (b) declare a SECOND named
   walker for the loop test (decl first of the walkers) and spell
   the loop `for (i = 0; cur2->idx != 0xFF; cur2++, i++)`.
231. **GOTO-THREADING ORPHAN = C-REACHABLE DEAD-B PAIR (s1-w27,
   gm_1A3F; extends the 16/17 dead-branch family)**: a target
   "missing dead b" pair is emitted when the original THREADS gotos:
   `goto not_found;` + `out: return var;` + tail
   `if (var != 0xFF) goto out;` — the hit-path direct return and the
   tail goto-out thread through out's `b`, leaving the orphan
   `b end` EMITTED.
232. **UNREACHABLE-AFTER-GOTO IS DELETED (s1-w27, negative; bounds
   96 and 231)**: MWCC DELETES statements unreachable after a goto —
   an unreachable-return spelling emits NOTHING; dead-b
   reconstruction must use REACHABLE threading (231), never
   unreachable source.
233. **BRANCH-SHADOW STORE-SETUP r0-MODE PARK FAMILY (s1-w27)**:
   multi-def store-feeding phi temps where ascending-first-fit gives
   ours r0 but the target shows r3 (or the call-result web processes
   before the expansion-diamond web, r26↔r27) — both colorings
   legal; 197/187-family MODE question, not decl-solvable. Members:
   gm_1A3F P1 (14 rows) + P2 (5 rows) · the whole grzakogenerator
   backlog (801CAC14/801CACB8/801CAEB0) · vi0501 un_8031D9F8.
   Open lever: r0-vs-dirty anon-temp mode enumeration (cracks the
   family jointly); one untested side-probe: gm_801A4CE0 u8→int
   proto re-type (needs gm_1A45.h owner + cross-TU bytegate).
234. **PARENT-INLINE WEB PROCESSES ABOVE NESTED-EXPANSION WEBS
   (s1-w27, itlinkarrow UnkMotion1_Anim 99.51 PARKED-WITH-PROOF)**:
   a parent inline's named web (decl-0 `Item* ip`) processes ABOVE
   the nested expansions' webs in EVERY C spelling — caller
   block-local ≡ expansion-named (extends 125), param-feed inert
   (187), 34-launder demotes exactly ONE slot (single-mutation bound
   151/195), and 215-style full anonymization EXPLODES (95.10/133r:
   VN does NOT merge across HSD calls — **anonymization requires
   CALL-FREE regions, bounds 215**). Only the target shows the
   inverted order — 197-class mode park; joins the itdraw-3B/
   count-v26 family.
235. **SPLIT-BOUNDARY JUMPTABLE ORPHANS ARE C-RECONSTRUCTABLE
   (datarecon-w27; toy .data 4.08→100.0 BYTE-IDENTICAL)**: a
   jumptable whose owner fn landed in ANOTHER split unit can be
   supplied as a synthetic `static void*` table of
   `(u8*)extern_fn + offset` entries (toy @992 over EXTERNAL
   un_80304B94) — bytes AND relocs match.
236. **0-IMPORTER NAMED POOL TWINS: LEAVE UNDEFINED (datarecon-w27;
   grpura .sdata2 83.3→100.0 CONTENT)**: when target-named slots are
   already occupied BYTE-IDENTICALLY by our anonymous pool dups
   (@519/520/563/564 = grPu_804DBA70..7C), defining the names
   ANYWHERE adds duplicate bytes (147 dup law) and by-name
   respelling regresses consumers (inline-arg const-vs-literal
   codegen, probed −1.5/−2.2 and reverted) — leave them UNDEFINED,
   converting one hard SIZE row into soft EXPORT-MISSING[0imp] rows.
237. **DECL-0 DIRECTION IS DEF-CLASS-SPLIT (lawapply-w28,
   REAL-FN-PROVEN on lbmemory 15320 probe d3; sharpens 189, confirms
   228's direction term)**: moving a web to decl-0 PROMOTES a
   PREAMBLE-def web (currentp r26→r27) and DEMOTES a MID-FN-def web
   (old r29→r26) — simultaneously, in ONE perm. Decl levers cannot
   move a mid-fn-def web band-front; only a def-site change could
   (and a guarded load is an illegal hoist).
238. **TOP SLOT IS DECL-UNCHALLENGEABLE (lawapply-w28, lbmemory
   probe d1 byte-INERT; bounds 226/228)**: the named multi-def
   first-statement web holding the band head (alloc r31) keeps it
   against ANY decl perm — even a preamble-def web at decl-0. The
   band head is structural, not decl-seeded.
239. **PARAM CS HOME IS DECL-DISPLACEABLE (lawapply-w28; bounds
   191)**: a param's callee-saved HOME register can be displaced by
   decl pressure (lbmemory handle home r28→r29 under d3) — 191's
   "params pinned" holds for the param's IDENTITY/order slot, not
   its home reg number.
240. **⭐⭐ LAW-PACK APPLICATION CLOSURE = PARK-PROVEN
   (lawapply-w28)**: when a causal law set (226-228) reconstructs a
   park's full register map EXACTLY and every law-implied lever is
   desk-checked/probed inert-or-anti-target, the park upgrades
   PARK-CONFIRMED → **PARK-PROVEN** and the lever line is CLOSED for
   that unit. Proven ×2: itdraw EECC (eff_abs=0 immutable, 227
   unfireable, 228 band empty; r29 needs emission-moving
   restructure) + lbmemory 15320 (d1/d3; {old band-front}+{currentp
   bottom} jointly C-UNREACHABLE under 171/189/190/191/226-228).
   Explanatory power ≠ constructive lever. **⚠ w29 BOUNDED**:
   upstream MATCHED itdraw EECC via the inline_sw switch-helper
   dedup (257) — a route OUTSIDE every probed class. PARK-PROVEN
   means "lever line closed within the AUDITED route classes",
   NEVER unit-level C-unreachability; dedup restructures that
   DELETE the web's defining statement escape the 226-228 frame.
   Before any future PARK-PROVEN verdict, desk-check 257-class
   restructures explicitly.
241. **⭐ V26 RELAXATION IS WHOLE-BODY-CONDITIONED — TRANSPORT ZERO
   (lawmodel-w27 probelog3, M-series; cand "230" there; sharpens
   227)**: the fresh-body one-interferer relaxation requires loop3b
   present AND a full-size wake-loop body AND terminal wake-loop
   position — EACH morph kills it independently (M1/M2/M3 all LOSS;
   M3 is web/body/W-constant, position-only). Not separable into
   web-count vs dormancy vs range-size. Replica-derived order rules
   are NON-PORTABLE: model parks only on body-matched replicas
   (probegen3 BODIES table = template); never spend park compiles on
   old-corpus rules.
242. **COUNT IS UNCONDITIONALLY PROMOTED ON ITS BODY — K_count IS
   BODY-LOCAL (lawmodel-w27 probelog3 E1; cand "231"; falsifies 228
   as count's mechanism)**: count@decl-0 with the winner band DEAD
   (v26 LOSS) still WINS r31; 12/12 fresh rows promote count
   regardless of decl position or band state. Old-body K_count=2
   does not transport — 229's trap instantiated on the fresh shape
   itself.
243. **ALWAYS-WINNER WEBS DO NOT FIRE THE 227 GATE (lawmodel-w27
   E1/D1/E2 triple; cand "232")**: count/tail-class always-winners
   interfere with v26's range but do NOT satisfy its
   interferer-prefix gate — only loop-web interferers (i2/w2/i5
   class) gate. Range overlap ≠ gate membership.
244. **DORMANCY COLLAPSE HANDS TOP COLOR TO THE NEW LONGEST RANGE
   (lawmodel-w27 M3, weak; cand "233-probe")**: when the wake-loop
   moves early (dormancy collapses), BASE — now the longest range —
   inherits r31. Supports a length-ranked promotion intension
   (promotion-at-minimal-prefix tracks range-length dominance +
   global pressure; decl-order atoms are tiebreaks only).
245. **NAMED-PTR-INTO-INDEXED-AGGREGATE ENABLES ZERO-INDEX
   CONST-PROP (s1-grind5-w28, won ftCo_Bury 08A0/0B20; cand "230"
   there; extends 121/149)**: `T* p = &agg->arr[0]` lets MWCC
   const-fold the index web (target li/mulli/addi/add = live
   `&fp->hurt_capsules[i]`); deleting the named ptr and spelling
   DIRECT indexed exprs is the ONLY form keeping the live index web.
246. **POOL-PINNED PAD FREEZE: `u8 _[N]={0}` POOLS AN N-BYTE ZERO
   .sdata2 ENTRY PER FN (s1-grind5-w28; cand "231")**: the pad's
   pool entry is score-load-bearing for SIBLINGS — never resize a
   pad in a pool-pinned TU (blocked ftCo_Bury 0C0D0C's 4+4
   pad-split). The PAD_STACK do{}while-UNUSED form never pools.
247. **INVENTED-VARARG-FLOAT AUDIT: crclr-vs-crset ⇒ CHECK ARG
   COUNT FIRST (s1-grind5-w28, won ftCo_Bury 0C0D0C; cand "232";
   complement of w27 ft_0892's missing-arg find)**: target `crclr
   cr1eq` = NO FP vararg — the decomp had INVENTED a 5th float arg
   to efSync_Spawn; removal also released the arg-precolor and fixed
   the f1/f2 FPR swap.
248. **BLOCK-DECL-WITH-CALL-INIT REMOVAL FROM A LOOP ARM = +1 @-id
   EACH (s1-grind5-w28, itkusudama zero-drift landing; cand "233";
   confirms 74's reverse-sign family at scale)**: fn-scope decl
   add/hoist = 0 drift; balance within the SAME fn using pad-swap −4
   / if(0) +2 (90 fn-entry binding). Measured shim balance landed 13
   reg rows at ZERO @-drift against 28 pins.
249. **A-VS-B LOOP UNIFICATION OF A MULTI-DEF WEB IS BYTE-EQUIVALENT
   (s1-grind5-w28, itkusudama; cand "234"; extends 125)**: a
   fn-scope multi-def web shared across two loops (target lwz-r0 +
   mr-r24 split = one `kind` web) may be spelled as either loop's
   form — unify freely; the decomp's invented per-loop block locals
   were the bug.
250. **SR OFFSET-ROVER VS POINTER-ROVER IS A MODE (s1-grind5-w28,
   negative; cand "235"; lbheap loop-2, 8 spellings falsified)**:
   mr-from-VN-node-init pointer-rover vs offset-rover is not
   C-spellable from the other side — a u32-walker reproduces the
   displacement folds but VN-merges the bumps. 191-immune; park
   class with the arena-web order.
251. **SQRTF LOCALSTATICS TICK THE SHARED @-COUNTER (datarecon-w27b;
   cand "A"; root-causes the w26 soundtest −2 open question)**:
   sqrtf expansion mints TU-shared localstatic @-ids; killing the
   call sites shifts ALL later @-ids by a uniform −2 (idiom-91
   link-irrelevant class). Cure when the target lacks _half/_three:
   TU-local plain-literal sqrtf (×2 proven).
252. **STRING-LITERAL-INITIALIZED char[] FORCES .data EVEN ≤8B
   (datarecon-w27b; cand "B"; bounds the w16 ≤8B law)**:
   `char x[] = "..."` lands in .data at any size — numeric u8
   brace-init is the ONLY .sdata spelling (grCn_804D4650).
253. **IDIOM-147 LIMIT: NAMED-DEF POOL-SLOT RETARGET FAILS ON
   RE-POOLED LITERALS (datarecon-w27b; cand "C")**: if the literal
   has OTHER plain uses later in the TU it re-pools — the cure must
   respell ALL uses or park.
254. **ORPHAN-WINDOW DETECTOR + UNCALLED-STATIC REFILL
   (datarecon-w27b; cand "D"; operationalizes 105/111)**:
   stream-position vs creator-lexical-order violations LOCATE
   deleted-creator pool orphans; idiom-111 uncalled static helpers
   refill them (×3 proven, incl. an f32-class orphan).
255. **CHAIN-LINKED STATIC RECORDS = ONE SELF-REFERENTIAL STATIC
   ARRAY (datarecon-w27b; cand "E")**: MWCC REJECTS forward static
   defs — reconstruct mutually-referencing static records as a
   SINGLE self-referential static array; dtk mid-stream gaps fold in
   as leading pad members (s16[10] {0,...} consumed an inferred
   0x4bc boundary).
256. **OVERLAY-STRUCT LAW (datarecon-w27b; cand "F")**: decomp
   whole-section overlay structs split into per-object defs; keep
   ONE `T* local = (T*)&head` base ptr for big-offset consumers
   (DDAC4) and use direct indexing where the target splits
   (E2738→100).
257. **⭐⭐ INLINE_SW SWITCH-HELPER DEDUP — SELF-DERIVED POINTER
   LOCAL (w29 upstream a8bc21722, MATCHED itdraw; extends 163,
   BOUNDS 240)**: factor a duplicated switch body into a
   static-inline VOID helper that re-derives the object pointer as
   its OWN local (`Item* ip = gobj->user_data;`), call it from both
   arms, DELETE the second arm's explicit reload (and any
   PAD_STACK the frame no longer needs). The named outer web moves
   into the inline-expansion band (163 + 215 composite), changing
   interference so the contested row recolors (itdraw E landed
   r29). 226-228/240 PARK-PROVEN verdicts cover band/order routes
   ONLY — dedup restructures that delete the web's defining
   statement escape the frame. Try on every hard-web park
   (gmopening 12B, gm_1A3F P1, grgreatbay 29B) before any
   C-unreachable verdict.
258. **VOLATILE-LOCALS MULTI-LITERAL POOL SEQUENCER (w29 upstream
   2eb67787b/20bdb91c5/00dab8087 ×3; industrializes 111/254)**: ONE
   uncalled static helper at TU top whose body declares `volatile
   f32/f64` locals initialized in the target pool order (returning
   their sum) pins the ENTIRE TU literal-pool order; volatile
   defeats const-fold/dedup so duplicate values (0.0f/1.0f) and
   mixed f32/f64 emit in declared order. Standard cure for the
   idiom-133 ".sdata2 POOL ORDER alone" linkcheck-fail class.
259. **`__declspec(section ".sdata2")` ON NON-CONST GLOBALS (w29
   upstream 2fdaddea3; guard `__MWERKS__ && !M2CTX`)**: forces
   byte-pattern coefficients into the pool section with natural lwz
   loads — sidesteps the idiom-24 const-fold dichotomy entirely;
   cleaner than deref-cast-of-const.
260. **U32-FIRST UNION BRACE-INIT (w29 upstream 2fdaddea3)**:
   reorder the union to `{u32 u; float f;}` so `= { 0x7FFFFFFF }`
   sets the bit pattern; read `.f` — typed-object spelling of
   F32_BIT_CAST, same bytes.
261. **PARAM SHADOW COPY RE-ARMED (w29 upstream 40f4c9d43, MATCHED
   ftCo_DamageFall in ONE line)**: `T copy = param;` consumed at a
   single call site splits the param web — a WORKING regalloc lever
   class. The falsified 25FAC probe was unit-local; never
   generalize one falsified probe to a dead class.
262. **PERMUTERSLOP DEAD NESTED NULL-CHECK (w29 upstream
   2eb67787b)**: `if (p == NULL) { if (p == NULL) { } }` emits the
   vestigial compare/branch present in the target — named lever for
   reproduce-dead-target-code (consistent with the 111-sublaw: MWCC
   has no post-codegen branch folding).
263. **VALUE-RETURNING INLINE + SETTER-AT-CALL-SITE / GETTER
   INLINES (w29 upstream 2eb67787b)**: convert `void Set*(gobj,m)`
   (compute AND set) into `f32 Get*(gobj,m)` fed to the setter at
   the call site — moves the jobj load/argument evaluation relative
   to the inline expansion; single-expression getter inlines shift
   web creation/scheduling.
264. **INTERLEAVED `u8 pad[N]` LOCALS MID-DECL-BAND (w29 upstream
   8b9026347, MATCHED itLinkarrow_UnkMotion1_Anim)**: explicit pad
   locals positioned BETWEEN named slots (`Vec3 a; u8 pad[4]; Vec3
   b; ...`) replace PAD_STACK — extends the trailing
   PAD_STACK↔u8-pad byte-neutral swap rule to positional mid-band
   placement. ⚠ in pool-pinned TUs idiom 246 still governs pad
   sizing.
265. **SMALLDATA ZERO-INIT/UNINIT EMITS REVERSE-DECL (w30 data-recon
   BOTH lanes; byte-proven gm_1884 + gm_1BA8 10-sym layout + ×2
   lane-B; merges lane-A cand-2 ≡ lane-B cand-A; sharpens the
   wave-3 .sbss rule into a layout-reconstruction recipe)**: MWCC
   emits uninitialized/zero-init smalldata globals in REVERSE
   lexical order (zero-INITIALIZED forward-class stays forward) —
   declare in reverse target order; u64 spelling 8-aligns slots,
   reproducing dtk gap-inclusive sizes.
266. **ZERO-INIT DEMOTION BEATS __declspec (w30 ×5 proven; merges
   lane-A cand-1 ≡ lane-B cand-B; extends 223 to
   .sdata2/f32/f64/GXColor)**: an all-zero scalar/struct falls to
   .sbss/.sbss2 EVEN under `__declspec(section ".sdata2")`. Cures:
   braced-zero ARRAY `[1]={0}` + `[0]` use rewrites, OR `const f32`
   def + idiom-147 deref-cast read (byte-identical lfs, gm_1884
   63C).
267. **.bss EMISSION = CODE-REF'D-IN-FN-ORDER, THEN REVERSE-DECL
   FLUSH of data-only-referenced syms (w30 gm_1BA8 9-sym probe)**:
   code refs PIN positions, so some target .bss orders are
   C-unreachable from the splitting TU; zero-content → the order

268. **@-TICK LEDGER (w30 gm_1884/gm_1BA8; idiom-91/48 class,
   canonical-report-INVISIBLE, strict-instrument-visible)**: named
   file-scope defs AND undefined-global first-references each
   consume @-counter ticks (symtab-creation order); killing a
   creator drifts ALL later @NNN names; named-def-for-anon swaps
   are drift-FREE (gm_1BA8 strings, net-zero).
269. **DTK SYNTHESIZES REAL RELOCS INSIDE "VALUE" DATA (w30
   lbcollision E4)**: small .sdata "value" bytes can be a relocated
   POINTER whose address bytes double as the value (E4 = GXColor*
   to un_80400080) — ALWAYS check target RELOCs before transcribing
   small .sdata values; `(T*)&ptr_slot` keeps the matched addi
   while the def carries the reloc.
270. **CROSS-SPLIT POOL IMPORTS ARE C-UNREACHABLE (w30 gm_1884
   @452_804DA600/@640_804DA5F8)**: target fns referencing a

271. **`__declspec(section ".rodata")` NON-CONST = FOLD-SIDESTEP
   FOR RELOC TABLES / Vec3 PACKS (w30 lane-B; extends 259 to

272. **POSITIONAL BLOB SPLIT (w30 grvenom 0x370 + grshrineroute
   0x4d0)**: dtk-merged blobs containing inline-header @-strings
   reconstruct BYTE-EXACTLY by splitting the defs AROUND the
   creator fns (defs before/after preserve stream position) — zero
   .text changes.
273. **UNCALLED u32→f32 HELPER PINS THE 4330 MAGIC AT .sdata2+0
   (w30 lane-B; applies 111/258 to COMPILER-GENERATED magics)**:

274. **OBJDIFF DISPLAY TRAPS ×2 (w30 lane-B; extends 45/91)**:
   defined-sym SDA21 record-offset flips AND @-counter shifts both
   FAKE fn DOWNs — the masked bytegate is the arbiter; 4 of

   @-display rows.
275. **MULTI-DEF FN-SCOPE VAR KILLS LOAD-STAGING (w30 hsd_3AA7
   fn_803B0E9C ×3 retry blocks)**: a single-def block-local
   `v=load` copy-props (lwz r0 + sunk mr); ONE fn-scope var
   re-assigned per block makes each def its own web — loads land

276. **COMMA-ASSIGN IN FOR-COND CARRIES INTO BODY WEBS (w30;
   matched-sibling fn_803ACD58 pattern, +5.5 on b0e9c)**:
   `for (...; (v = a/b) cond; ...)` with the IDENTICAL full div
   expression in cond + body-top CSEs across the boundary —
   re-evaluated loop-cond division spelling.
277. **CTR-LOOP TELL (w30 grGreens_80215D54, +8.9)**: m2c's
   `count = N - i; if (i < N) do {...} while (--count)` scaffold ==
   a mtctr/bdnz counted `for` — re-roll as plain
   `for (r = row; r < N; r++)`.
278. **2D-INDEXING RE-DERIVATION (w30 grgreens ×3; extends 30)**:
   direct `arr[i][j]` re-derives the address per statement (stores
   kill mem-CSE) and folds `[r-1]` as a rover−stride displacement;
   inline-getter POINTER TEMPS instead mint separate mulli webs and
   collapse lbzx — kill the temps, index directly.
279. **NEIGHBOR-PAIR ANCHOR (w30 grGreens_802150C4, +21.1)**:
   paired `[r±1][c±1].fld` call args CSE to ONE `[r][c]` anchor
   with negative displacements under direct indexing.
280. **ABS-MERGE COALESCE SPELLING (w30 ftNn_Init; extends 109c)**:
   `if ((v = a - b) < 0.0f) v = -v;` coalesces the abs merge-web —
   the plain two-statement spelling emits an fmr-split. Probe other
   fmr-split abs sites.
281. **ADDRESS-TAKEN-LOCAL STORE BARRIER (w30 ftNn_Init)**:
   per-component `a.x = b.x + (c.x - d.x)` cannot interleave PAST
   stores to an address-taken local — dx/dy/dz temps declared
   before the first store restore the target schedule.
282. **PAD_STACK-BEFORE-INNER-BLOCK = PAD-ABOVE-LOCALS LEVER (w30
   struct-B; complements 264)**: PAD_STACK placed before an inner
   block positions pad ABOVE the block's locals — the
   statement-macro workaround for positional padding.
283. **BLOCK-LOCAL INITIALIZED ARRAY = IN-BRANCH TEMPLATE COPY (w30
   groldyoshi F088)**: block-local `int arr[N] = {...}` emits
   in-branch lis/addi + a two-reg interleaved template copy (the
   template sym pairs as [.rodata-0]) vs file-scope-const element
   copies — a binary tell for block-local vs file-scope const
   arrays. Naming queue: grOy_803B83F0 → local-template.
284. **GUARD-LEGALIZED PREAMBLE DEF (w30 adoption; sharpens
   257/228)**: a static-inline VOID helper called INSIDE a guard
   turns a "hoist-illegal" guarded load into a helper-preamble
   init-decl → band-front placement with zero emission moves
   (lbmemory lb2, grgreatbay g6-g7) — the cure for "old band-front
   needs an illegal hoist" park rows.
285. **INLINE-PASS COPY-FOLD WALL (w30 lbmemory 15320; generalizes
   257's deletion to a wall class)**: inside inlined bodies MWCC
   deletes pure statement-level local copies (`src=old`)
   UNCONDITIONALLY — `register`, 263-getter, comma, decl-order
   cannot retain the at-statement addi (6 spellings falsified).
   Open hunt: a copy-retaining construct; until found this is the
   single wall on lbmemory 15320 (ONE row from match).
286. **BLOCK-CONFINED WEBS = BOTTOM PHASE, REGISTER AND STACK (w30
   adoption; lbmemory lb11 trio + ftCo a5/d3-d4)**: block decls
   allocate in the bottom band; a PAD_STACK block array ALWAYS
   takes the LOWEST slot — to pad anywhere use fn-scope
   `UNUSED u8 _pad[N]` positionally per 264.
287. **STACK SLOTS = DESCENDING DECL ORDER (w30 ftCo_0A01 a2/d3)**:
   first decl gets the HIGHEST slot; with 264/286 this landed three
   exact stack bands.
288. **EXPRESSION-FED HELPER PARAMS BEAT PARENT-PARAM IDENTITY (w30
   grgreatbay CRACK → FLIP #16)**: feeding an all-param inline
   helper EXPRESSIONS (`gobj->hsd_obj, gobj, GET_GROUND(gobj)`)
   escapes order/frame walls that parent-param identity hits — no
   196-Kongo frame charge in all-param form; mutation-frame
   impossibility proofs are FRAME-LOCAL. Bound: wholesale
   whole-body factoring also rotates anchors (lbmemory FC8 f1).
289. **@-IDs ARE BB-LABEL-COUPLED (w30 ftCo_0A01; bounds 41)**:
   deleting branches shifts TU @-numbering (BB-label creation);
   scalar/array decl shims mint NOTHING — the @-row cost of a
   structural edit is branch-count-driven and link-neutral.
290. **ALWAYS RE-DERIVE, NEVER RESTORE (w30 exec symbols-truth
   fix)**: ground truth for a stale symbols.txt line is the WINNING
   src object's EMISSION, never "our old value" — upstream's own
   @-numbering was stale vs their own src (kirby @269→@274 restored
   by derivation; ftcolanim needed a THIRD value, neither ours nor
   kept).
291. **STALE-LINE TRIAGE SPLITS 3-WAY (w30 exec; 75 fix / 19
   already-true / 8 no-ground-truth)**: "stale line" ≠ "ours was
   right"; where src emits no symbol yet there is NO ground truth —
   keep; demote globals to @-pin locals only after a 0-importer
   proof across ALL link inputs.
292. **SWITCH JTBLS QUEUE AND FLUSH AT THE NEXT FILE-SCOPE DATA DEF
   (w31 ftkirby; extends 235 + the w30 gm_1884 jt-sandwich)**:
   consecutive switch fns' jtbls emit as ONE band in fn order at the
   next file-scope data def's stream slot (or EOF); arrangement is
   law-agnostic with defs placed around fns (sandwich) — ×6 jtbls
   byte-proven landing 0x1d64-0x1fa0 exactly. Decls/typedefs are
   emission-neutral; defs emit at their lexical slot.
293. **DEF-VISIBILITY CODEGEN COUPLING WORKS IN BOTH DIRECTIONS (w31
   cross-lane merge: ftkirby cand-b ≡ grbigblue cand-B; extends
   104 + 224)**: when the target compiled fns BEFORE their data,
   extern-decl + later/EOF def is the *codegen-true* arrangement —
   visible defs CHANGE .text (EED50 312B w/ visible defs vs 332B ==
   target w/ extern decls; EEC34 276→288 vs tgt 284) and an in-TU
   .rodata def before its uses folds multi-access anchors to ONE
   section-anchored base (grbigblue 8120/8134, −1.16pp measured);
   EOF-def keeps uses extern-shaped per-use lis while still emitting
   the bytes. Decomp "defs up top" can silently break .text.
294. **OURS-BIGGER DIAGNOSIS RECIPE (w31 ftkirby, first-of-class)**:
   ours-only syms whose NAMES carry another TU's address range =
   pre-split monolith leftovers (verify 0-ref, delete); ours-only
   @-anon jtbls at the .data tail whose entries point into in-TU fns
   = duplicated transcribed jumptables (fake case-label pseudo-decls
   occupy the target band — delete defs, let the switches mint, then
   sandwich per 292). Likely campaign-wide in pre-split monoliths.
295. **SIZED STATIC TENTATIVE + INIT DEF REJECTED (w31 gmresult;
   extends 70 beyond unsized arrays)**: MWCC rejects
   `static char x[10];` followed by an initialized def ("object
   redefined") — cure = non-static extern decl + EOF def.
296. **IMPOSSIBLE-SANDWICH READING (w31 gmresult; extends 272)**: a
   target data def positioned BETWEEN two literals minted by the
   SAME fn proves the original spelled those "literals" as named
   file-scope defs — forces the all-defs route for the section.
297. **EXPORT-NAMED DEAD-POSITION DEFS (w31 grbigblue)**: naming a
   dead-position string def by the target export name
   (grBb_803E2EB8) clears the EXPORT row AND lets folded sym+offset
   reads (sym+0x158) match target reloc records.
298. **STATIC IS PLACEMENT-LOAD-BEARING FOR SMALL STRING char[] (w31
   leak; bounds 139, refines 252)**: global string char[] forces
   .data at ANY size; static ≤8B string char[] stays .sdata —
   dropping `static` moved HSD_Leak_804D6000/4 .sdata→.data and
   reshaped sda21 consumer loads (97.30→94.82). 139's "sections stay
   byte-identical" holds only for non-sda sections.
299. **VOLATILE IS LOAD-BEARING IN FLOAT-ONLY 258 SEQUENCERS (w31
   gm_1A4C — first in-campaign 258 application)**: the non-volatile
   (itzako-style) sequencer form CONST-FOLDS float-only chains (sum
   literal pooled instead of the operand literals); volatile locals
   force each literal into the pool in sequence. Related park note:
   `#pragma dont_reuse_strings` mints a copy PER SITE — it cannot
   spell one shared dup pair (the sislib/particle/gm_1A4C dup-string
   class stays UNCRACKED; mid-file #include is NOT a pool barrier).
300. **FLOAT-HOME LAW (w31 struct-tier)**: MWCC reserves 4B stack
   homes per INITIALIZED fn-block float beyond the first, rounded
   to 8 (5→16B / 3→8B / 2→0B); inner-block inits get none — tune
   frame size with init-count + dual positional pads.
301. **LOAD-SEEDED op= COALESCES CLAMP WEBS (w31; extends 109c)**:
   `n3 = x; n3 += n2;` coalesces def+phi of a clamp web and kills
   the fmr split; ternary, explicit-abs and decl-order spellings all
   falsified.
302. **NAMED ROW-ARRAY LOCAL FORCES lfs-DISPLACEMENT (w31; extends
   278)**: a named `float (*arr)[3]` local + `arr[i][-k]` indexing
   emits add + lfs-displacement; M2C_FIELD/inline-cast spellings
   fold to subi+lfsx — the NAMED local is load-bearing.
303. **extlwi+srawi = MWCC s16 BITFIELD TEMPLATE (w31)**: extlwi
   13,20 + srawi 20 is the template for `s16 pad:4; s16 angle:12;`
   — hand shift-pair spellings emit slwi instead (falsified ×3).
304. **f30/f31 CALLEE-SAVED FPR PAIRING IS DECL-ORDER-DRIVEN (w31)**:
   FPR pair assignment follows declaration order (GPR/volatile
   decl-order stays inert per w30) — a decl-order lever exists for
   FPR pairs specifically.
305. **SIGNATURE RECOVERY FROM CALLERS (w31 Item_8026AD20)**: a
   callee's true signature can carry UNUSED trailing params proven
   only by caller-side arg loads (li r6 = 1/0 by branch); adding
   them to a Matching unit's definition is byte-invariant (item.o
   sha256 EXACT) — recover signatures from callers, then freeze.
306. **jumptable_→@ RENAMES ARE DOL-BYTE-SAFE (w31 naming-16, ×5
   probe-proven)**: dtk jt relocs survive the rename — settles the
   old "jtbl→@ config-recoverability" queue item and unlocks
   jumptable-park naming.
307. **SIBLING-ISOLATED DOL GATE = `ninja build/GALE01/ok` (w31
   naming-16; extends 103)**: the DOL+dtk CHECK target survives
   broken sibling non-linked units — full-ninja EXIT CODE is NOT a
   valid gate on a live shared tree (exit-2 ×2 attributed to sibling
   mid-edits of non-DOL-dep units).
308. **TRANSIENT PGREP GHOSTS (w31 naming-16; sharpens 211 + the w29
   ps-p rule)**: pgrep can hit pids already dead — the guard is
   wait-for-idle (capped) + `ps -p` RESAMPLE, not abort-on-hit.
309. **POSITIONAL @-ALIGNMENT IS UNSAFE; CONTENT-ORDER LCS KEYING IS
   THE INSTRUMENT (w31 naming-16)**: where ours-extra pool slots
   shift offsets, positional alignment value-MIS-PAIRS pins (grvenom
   .sdata2 would have been WRONG ×4) — derive @-renumber maps by
   content-byte LCS over target-vs-src objects, then value-verify
   every pin.
310. **IMPORT→LITERAL INVERSION DIAGNOSIS (w32 tylist; the INVERSE of
   the leak/gmresult import→def class)**: when target data-section
   stream order == reloc FIRST-USE order across ALL entries, the
   un_*/symbols.txt names on pool entries are dtk artifacts
   (scope:global boilerplate), NOT defs — respell the decomp's extern
   imports as PLAIN LITERALS; the named-twin EXPORT rows go
   [0imp]-soft per 236.
311. **EQUALITY-COMPARE CONST-FIRST CANONICALIZATION IS A RECON TELL
   (w32 tylist un_80313508; operationalizes 92)**: MWCC canonicalizes
   literal ==/!= compares CONST-FIRST in fcmpu; a target row with the
   VARIABLE first proves the original read a NAMED symbol — keep or
   restore the extern at exactly those sites (pool entry stays anon;
   the import resolves to the same address at link).
312. **>8B STRUCT DEF LEAVES SMALLDATA (w32 mnmainrule 4B88; bounds
   223)**: a dtk-merged .sdata run CANNOT be respelled as one >8B
   struct (it goes .data); re-splitting into ≤8B scalar defs is the
   only .sdata spelling.
313. **u8 ARRAYS 4-ALIGN IN .sdata, SCALARS PACK (w32 ifstatus color
   quads; refines the gmresult u8-block law)**: 1B+3B dtk symbol
   boundaries are NOT def boundaries; u8[4] quads are the packed
   spelling.
314. **ONE-PAST-END POINTER ARG = RELOC-FREE addi INTO THE NEXT
   OBJECT (w32 ifstatus 802F7134 →100)**: a target
   `addi rX,rAnchor,sizeof(arr)` with NO reloc, landing inside an
   adjacent dead-static string, is the `(char*)(ptr_local + N)`
   one-past-the-end spelling; the pointer LOCAL pins sym-anchored
   materialization (98/256); a visible-def with plain indexing
   section-anchors at .data+0 instead (293); an EOF def kills both
   the fold and the stream (all three probed).
315. **SEQUENCERS MINT .data STRINGS TOO (w32 ifstatus; extends 258
   beyond .sdata2)**: `char* volatile s = "...";` locals in the
   TU-top uncalled static sequencer pin .data pool-STRING order;
   floats and strings can share ONE helper.
316. **POINTER-TYPED BYTE-PATTERN DEF (w32 mnmainrule)**: `u8* x =
   (u8*)0x02030506;` under __declspec(section ".sdata2") emits the
   byte pattern reloc-free (abs init) and stays decl-compatible with
   a `u8*` extern.
317. **MULTI-TU BAND DIAGNOSIS + ALL-DEFS RECONSTRUCTION — THE
   DUP-POOL RECIPE (w32 ftCo_Attack100, class CRACKED; extends
   294/296)**: symbols.txt-named b=0 .sdata2/.sdata slots in
   same-VALUE groups keyed to fn clusters = per-original-TU pool
   epochs of a pre-dtk-merge multi-TU band (dup 0.0/1.0 pairs,
   "jobj.h"/"jobj" ×5, etc.). Reconstruct via named const defs at the
   exact stream slots — def-AFTER-readers for plain reads (itdrop
   precedent), volatile casts where the def precedes the reader —
   plus the few TRUE anon mints pinned by lexical def/fn-order
   interleave (move the creator fn to target order so the FIRST
   converter pins the 4330-magic mint); ATTRIBUTE_ALIGN(8) reproduces
   per-epoch pads; TU-local inline clones (gmresult-76F60 precedent)
   re-key __assert pool args to the named arrays; char[4]="" fillers
   (320) supply the .data epoch pads. The gm_1A4C/sislib dup-pair
   class IS this in miniature — the named-def route SIDESTEPS the
   uncracked dup-pool mechanism entirely. Residual limit: TRUE anon
   magics deduped by our single TU stay parked (magic-dedupe class).
318. **CONST-PROP/HOIST LADDER FOR NAMED CONST READS (w32 Attack100;
   extends 104)**: plain named read = CSE+const-prop (needs
   def-after-reader) · `*(f32*)&const_obj` = hoistable ACROSS CALLS
   into a callee-saved · `*(volatile T*)&sym` = load pinned at the
   statement, one load per cast. MWCC arg eval order is
   LEFT-to-RIGHT (byte-proven): an `(z = *(volatile f32*)&X)`
   assignment-in-arg + later `z` arg reuse reproduces post-call
   lfs+fmr exactly (86E0 →100).
319. **VOLATILE-LOCAL FAN-OUT (w32 Attack100 8C54/A8E4 →100)**:
   `f32 z = *(volatile f32*)&X;` + N uses = exactly ONE load feeding
   stores and args byte-exactly.
320. **ZERO-INIT DEMOTION BOUNDS (w32; refines 252/266/298)**:
   char[4]="" (all-zero string init) demotes to .sdata even
   NON-static (252's .data-forcing needs nonzero content), and
   `__declspec(section ".data")` DOES beat zero-init demotion for
   char[] — unlike floats/.sdata2, where demotion WINS over declspec
   (mpisland P1: declspec'd .sdata2 float=0.0F lands in .sbss2).
   char[]+declspec is the .data zero-pad instrument.
321. **VOLATILE BELONGS ON THE READ, NEVER THE DEF (w32 mpisland P2
   falsification)**: a `volatile const` DEF keeps the named symbol
   placed but makes MWCC copy ALL reads through a fresh anon pool
   slot (+11 rt=109 relocs, .text +4B); spell volatility as
   `*(volatile const T*)&sym` at the read sites (sobjlib.c:632
   in-tree precedent).
322. **u32-ONLY ZERO-TRIP-GUARD FOLD ON UNROLLED CONSTANT-TRIP LOOPS
   (w32 s1 gm_801A9630 +1.46)**: MWCC drops the dead li/cmpwi/bge
   entry guard on an unrolled constant-trip loop ONLY for UNSIGNED
   counters; s32 keeps it. Falsified: do-while (kills the unroll),
   (s32) cast in the cond (kills the unroll), init-outside/while/
   ptr-deref (no-ops), PAD_STACK (orthogonal).
323. **STORES KILL POINTER-CHAIN CSE — RE-DEREF PER STATEMENT (w32 s1
   gm_801BBB64 +3.54; extends the 235-era alias rules)**: MWCC
   re-derives a pointer chain after EVERY store; decomp
   `p = chain; ... use p` caching is the anti-pattern. Write the
   chain expression per statement — a condition's load IS reused for
   the first store-free field read, then reloaded after stores.
324. **NAMED-LOCAL vs CSE-TEMP COLORING LOTTERY (w32 s1 ftKb EED50
   →100; third sighting incl. the 9630 homes web)**: single-init
   pointer locals take scratch-r3-then-home-copy; multi-assign goes
   direct-into-home; a pure CSE expression (no named local)
   coalesces straight into the precolored arg reg (kills
   `addi rX,r3,0`), and flips two-colorings + call-arg schedules.
   Choosing named-local vs inline-expression IS the lever for this
   residual S1 class. Sibling law same session: an s8→u8 ELEMENT
   re-type fixes extsb rows where a (u8) CAST falsified (EEC34 →100
   — the TYPE form reaches both load sites, the cast only one).
325. **fn-PTR-CAST CALLS FORCE INDIRECT lis/addi/mtlr (w32 s1
   gm_1884)**: casting a direct callee
   `((void(*)(int,int))fn)(...)` to shim a proto mismatch forces an
   indirect call — a FALSIFIER for proto-mismatch shims; fix the
   prototype at the owning header instead (HEADER QUEUE: gm_16AE.h:52
   + gm_16AE.c:289 fn_8016B388 s16→int, gm_16AE bytegate expected
   identical — callee-side s16 params are caller-converted; verify).

### Experiment results (wave 3)

- **BSS/sbss ordering rule (8-compile evidence)**: statics allocate at their
  DECLARATION point; extern .bss (>8B) at FIRST CODE REFERENCE (sub-function
  granular); unreferenced externs flush at end-of-TU in REVERSE decl order;
  extern .sbss (<=8B) always flushes reverse-decl regardless of use; size
  and alignment never matter. Lever for wrong order: make the early-target
  symbol `static` (if only locally referenced) or reorder first uses.
- **Naming rows**: objdiff pairs relocs by exact name OR identical
  section-relative address — so naming rows appear ONLY when offsets drift
  (e.g. duplicated sqrtf localstatic literals shift .sdata2). Fix: name the
  target symbols in config/GALE01/symbols.txt (split re-runs via ninja,
  ~1.2s). Procedure documented in wave-3 report; FRAGILE: '@N' indices
  shift when src edits change literal pools — re-verify after src changes.
  Big targets: mnCharSel_802640A0 (154 naming rows), toy.c fns (65-146).

### Known-hard regalloc patterns (decl-order rule does NOT reach these)

- **Induction-temp pairs**: when the swap is between a user variable and a
  compiler-created strength-reduction temp (roving pointer), declaration
  shuffles shift OTHER registers and usually make fuzzy worse. Example:
  lbDvd_CachePreloadedFile (99.62) — failed: i-first, i-last, init-at-decl,
  loop-shadowed block-local (98.1-99.4). Matched siblings in the same file
  use the identical loop shape, so the lever is in this function's pre-loop
  variable mix. Needs systematic enumeration (wave-3 dedicated agent).
- **Inline-copy pairs**: under #pragma inline_depth(N), inlined callees
  instantiate their locals per level; a swap between level-1 and level-2
  copies is controlled by the CALLEE's body, not the caller's. Editing the
  callee risks breaking its own (matched) function + other inline sites.
  Example: lb_80011C18 (99.69) — mismatch entirely inside inlined
  lb_80011B74 tail. Failed: init-at-decl. Treat as expert tier.
